Instead of following conventional approaches, we identify two distinct hallucination patterns-Perceptual-Semantic Dissociation and Localized Fixation-and propose FLASH (Frequency-Localized Attention SHaping), a training-free and CD-free framework. FLASH utilizes a Spectral Vortex Score to detect vision heads within multi-head attention layers and applies adaptive spectral modulation to rectify the visual information flow during decoding. Empirical results demonstrate that FLASH achieves a superior balance between performance and efficiency compared to SOTA methods.

</details>


### [137] [UniAfford: Token-Routed Multitask Learning for Generalizable 2D-3D Affordance Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37264)
*Yuhao Liu,Yiming Zhong,Hanqing Wang,Shaocheng Yan,Yuhang Zhang,Wenzhou Lyu,Ziyang Ding,Wei Zhang,Xue Zhao,Jin Pan,Yuexin Ma,Xinge Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Token Router for Tasks多任务训练范式，并实例化为UniAfford统一框架与UniAfford-Data数据集，实现可泛化的2D-3D可供性感知，在零样本跨模态基准上取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2D与3D可供性定位长期作为独立问题发展，任务定义、监督形式、数据集与评测协议各不相同，导致跨视觉与几何空间的可迁移物体-可供性语义难以学习，这种割裂限制了统一表征的构建。

Method: 提出Token Router for Tasks范式，将上下文隐状态路由至任务专用分支，无需语言头生成预定义标记，路由状态由分支特定目标直接监督，使稠密预测损失能塑造共享MLLM表征。实例化为UniAfford：以MLLM为共享语义枢纽，配模态感知token路由器生成图像与点云可供性查询，分别驱动SAM式像素解码器与SONATA式点解码器，支持仅图像、仅点云或配对多模态输入的2D、3D及联合推理。配套UniAfford-Data数据集，在共享物体-可供性分类体系下整合像素级2D、点级3D标注与语言指令，通过语义级2D-3D配对支持异构监督。

Result: 在无需目标域微调的情况下，于2D与3D可供性基准上展现强零样本泛化能力；在模态隔离协议下取得各分支最先进性能。消融实验验证了token路由、联合2D-3D监督与解码器耦合的有效性，语言头诊断表明路由后的隐状态携带了有意义的物体-可供性语义。

Conclusion: 该工作通过统一的多任务训练与路由机制，弥合了2D与3D可供性感知的割裂，实现了跨模态、可泛化的可供性定位，并公开了数据与项目页面。

Abstract: Affordance perception aims to localize actionable regions supporting embodied interaction, yet 2D and 3D affordance grounding have evolved as separate problems, with different task definitions, supervision formats, datasets, and evaluation protocols. This fragmentation limits the learning of transferable object-affordance semantics across visual and geometric spaces. We propose Token Router for Tasks, a multitask training paradigm for MLLM-based systems that routes contextual hidden states to task-specific branches without requiring the language head to generate predefined markers. Routed states are supervised directly by branch-specific objectives, enabling dense prediction losses to shape shared MLLM representations. We instantiate this paradigm as UniAfford, a unified framework for generalizable 2D-3D affordance perception, together with UniAfford-Data, a dataset integrating pixel-level 2D annotations, point-level 3D annotations, and language instructions under a shared object-affordance taxonomy, supporting heterogeneous supervision through semantic-level 2D-3D pairing. UniAfford adopts an MLLM as a shared semantic hub and a modality-aware token router to produce image- and point-cloud-affordance queries. These queries respectively condition a SAM-style pixel decoder and a SONATA-based point decoder, enabling flexible 2D, 3D, and joint affordance inference from image-only, point-cloud-only, or paired multimodal inputs. Experiments demonstrate strong zero-shot generalization across 2D and 3D affordance benchmarks without target-specific fine-tuning, alongside state-of-the-art branch-wise performance under modality-isolated protocols. Ablations validate token routing, joint 2D-3D supervision, and decoder coupling, while language-head diagnostics show that routed latent states carry meaningful object-affordance semantics. Project page: https://4dvlab.github.io/UniAfford

</details>


### [138] [SAM Meets VLM: Parameter-Decoupled Full-Parameter Training for Unified Medical Reasoning and Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37283)
*Xuyang Cao,Enyou Liu,Jun Zhao,Zhuoyun Liu,Jintao Fei,Leo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出参数解耦训练框架，统一医学多模态推理与分割，实现语言条件分割同时保持推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学多模态大模型不仅要回答临床问题，还需定位预测所依据的视觉证据。常见做法是用<SEG> token把VLM与SAM式分割连接，但图像级推理与像素级分割对共享表示空间要求不同，全参数训练的统一架构难以兼顾两者。

Method: 采用参数解耦训练框架：将<SEG>隐状态作为掩码解码器的语义到空间提示，并鼓励其与通用语言状态可分离，减少歧义分割提示与对推理表示的干扰。分为三步：先做医学浅层对齐，在不干扰LLM的情况下让视觉特征适配临床语言；再进行受控指令微调，塑造可分离的<SEG>提示状态，以Davies-Bouldin指数（DBI）监控，同时缩放进入语言主干的以分割梯度；最后冻结VLM，专门训练SAM分支以提升掩码精度而不改变推理参数。

Result: 在医学指代分割、grounding、视觉问答与文本问答基准上，该框架在实现强语言条件分割的同时保持了有竞争力的推理能力。消融实验表明两阶段指令微调、梯度缩放和分割专门化均有贡献。

Conclusion: 参数解耦训练可有效缓解统一架构中推理与分割的表示冲突，是统一医学推理与分割的可行方案。

Abstract: Medical multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly expected not only to answer clinical questions, but also to localize the visual evidence behind their predictions. A common strategy connects a vision--language model (VLM) with SAM-style segmentation through a special <SEG> token, yet full-parameter training of this unified architecture is difficult because image-level reasoning and pixel-level segmentation impose different requirements on the shared representation space. To address this issue, we propose a parameter-decoupled training framework for unified medical reasoning and segmentation. The framework treats the <SEG> hidden state as a semantic-to-spatial prompt for the mask decoder and encourages it to become separable from generic language states, reducing ambiguous segmentation prompts and potential disruption to reasoning representations. It first performs medical shallow alignment to adapt visual features to clinical language without disturbing the LLM; then controlled instruction tuning shapes separable <SEG> prompt states, monitored by the Davies--Bouldin Index (DBI), while scaling segmentation gradients entering the language backbone; finally, the SAM branch is specialized with the VLM frozen to improve mask precision without altering reasoning parameters. Experiments on medical referring segmentation, grounding, visual QA, and textual QA benchmarks show that our framework achieves strong language-conditioned segmentation while preserving competitive reasoning ability. Ablations show that two-phase instruction tuning, gradient scaling, and segmentation specialization all contribute to the model.

</details>


### [139] [VISTA-Bench: Benchmarking Multilingual Image Translation with Image-Specific Rubrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37287)
*Bo Lv,Mao Zheng,Zheng Li,Fangxu Liu,Mingrui Sun,Tao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出VISTA-Bench，一个覆盖22种语言、10个领域的图像翻译评测基准，并设计了图像专属的评分标准评估协议，对16个主流模型进行了系统评测与分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像翻译是多模态模型面向多语言应用的基础能力，需要跨语言的视觉理解与语义保持。然而现有基准语言覆盖有限，且常缺乏明确的图像专属评估标准，难以全面评估该能力。

Method: 提出VISTA-Bench，覆盖22种语言和10个领域，并开发图像专属评分标准评估协议。基准通过采样保证语言与场景覆盖，并结合模型辅助、人工核验的标注，将相关文本归组为连贯语义单元，提供多语言参考译文。评分标准明确必要内容、语义关系和可接受的翻译变体，针对翻译质量与视觉及知识依赖信息的保持分别给出基于输出的分数。

Result: 对16个主流模型（12个多模态模型与4个纯文本输入模型）进行了广泛评测，并在语言、领域和评估维度上提供了系统分析。

Conclusion: VISTA-Bench为图像翻译能力提供了覆盖面更广、评估标准更明确的系统评测方案，并通过多模型、多维度分析揭示该能力的现状。

Abstract: Image translation is a fundamental capability of multimodal models for multilingual applications, requiring visual understanding and meaning preservation across languages. However, existing benchmarks have limited language coverage and often lack explicit image-specific evaluation criteria, making it difficult to comprehensively assess this capability. To systematically evaluate this capability, we introduce VISTA-Bench, covering 22 languages and 10 domains, and develop an image-specific rubric evaluation protocol. The benchmark combines sampling for language and scenario coverage with model-assisted, human-verified annotations that group related text into coherent semantic units and provide multilingual reference translations. The rubrics specify essential content, semantic relations, and acceptable translation variants, yielding separate output-based scores for translation quality and the preservation of visual and knowledge-dependent information. We conduct extensive evaluations of 16 mainstream models, including 12 multimodal models and four text-input models, and provide systematic analyses across languages, domains, and evaluation dimensions.

</details>


### [140] [Technical note on: Zero-Training Feature-Space Alignment via Information Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37302)
*Behraj Khan,Tahir Qasim Syed,Syed Ahmad Chan Bukhari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出零训练费舍尔几何对齐（ZFGA），一种闭式、无需修改模型参数的测试时适应方法，通过对齐特征空间的费舍尔几何提升协变量偏移下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度视觉模型在分布偏移下常出现性能退化；现有测试时适应方法通常需要迭代优化、超参数调优及多次前向-反向传播，成本高且不稳定。作者希望找到一种轻量、确定性的替代方案。

Method: ZFGA基于"分布偏移会扭曲特征空间几何"这一观察。它估计预测分布对特征嵌入的费舍尔信息矩阵，并施加线性变换，使测试特征的费舍尔几何与从干净数据计算得到的参考几何对齐，相当于在特征空间进行一次自然梯度启发的预条件处理；仅需前向传播与矩阵运算。

Result: 在CIFAR-10-C和ImageNet-C上，使用ResNet-50、DINO ViT-S/16、CLIP ViT-B/32评估。ZFGA在三模型上均优于零样本推理，但并非每个模型上最强：ResNet-50上协方差白化更好，CLIP上费舍尔白化与ZFGA无统计差异。在六种训练无关与基于梯度的对比方法中，ZFGA是唯一未对三类模型家族造成实质损害的方法。费舍尔几何扭曲与ZFGA增益正相关（Pearson r=0.366，p=0.017）。

Conclusion: 几何错位是鲁棒性退化的一个成因；ZFGA作为无需训练、确定性、轻量的闭式测试时适应方法，可作为优化类方法的替代方案。

Abstract: Deep vision models often degrade under distribution shift. Test-time adaptation can improve robustness but typically requires iterative optimization, hyperparameter tuning, and multiple forward-backward passes. We propose Zero-Training Fisher Geometry Alignment (ZFGA), a closed-form method that improves robustness under covariate shift without modifying model parameters. ZFGA is based on the observation that distribution shifts distort feature-space geometry. It estimates the Fisher information matrix of the predictive distribution with respect to feature embeddings and applies a linear transformation that aligns test-feature Fisher geometry with a reference geometry computed from clean data. This provides a natural-gradient-inspired preconditioning step in feature space. We evaluate ZFGA on CIFAR-10-C and ImageNet-C using ResNet-50, DINO ViT-S/16, and CLIP ViT-B/32. ZFGA consistently improves over zero-shot inference across all three models, although it is not the strongest method for every model. Covariance whitening performs better on ResNet-50, while Fisher whitening is statistically indistinguishable from ZFGA on CLIP. Across six training-free and gradient-based alternatives (covariance whitening, Fisher whitening, TENT, T3A, LAME, and AdaNPC), ZFGA is the only method that does not substantially harm any of the three model families. The Fisher geometry distortion is also positively correlated with ZFGA gain (Pearson r = 0.366, p = 0.017), providing preliminary evidence that geometric misalignment contributes to robustness degradation. ZFGA requires only forward passes and matrix operations at inference time, offering a lightweight and deterministic alternative to optimization-based test-time adaptation.

</details>


### [141] [FLASH: A "Generate Once, Synthesize Many" Framework for Synthetic Anomaly Generation in Industrial Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37314)
*Abhay Kumar Das,Rajesh Gangireddy,Ashwin Vaidya,Samet Akcay*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FLASH提出"一次生成、多次合成"范式，将缺陷生成与异常合成解耦，仅用正常图像即可高效生成多样化工业异常数据，在MVTec AD 2上性能接近真实缺陷且速度提升超11.95倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业异常数据集中真实缺陷稀缺或难以获取，需要合成异常来扩充数据。现有方法处于两个极端：流程化方法速度快但难以表达复杂异常；生成式方法能产生多样缺陷但需昂贵的逐样本生成成本。

Method: FLASH将缺陷生成与异常合成解耦，采用"一次生成、多次合成"范式。仅给定正常图像，先用视觉语言模型（VLM）引导和图像生成模型产生少量缺陷图像，从中提取、验证并存储可复用的缺陷补丁；合成时，对象边界抑制（OBS）识别宿主图像的物体感知前景区域，多分辨率光谱金字塔（MRSP）噪声生成多样且尺寸可控的掩码以确定缺陷位置和空间范围；随后定位缺陷区域、采样尺寸可控的放置掩码并将检索到的缺陷无缝融合到新的无缺陷图像上，无需再次图像生成即可构建大规模多样合成异常集。

Result: 在MVTec AD 2数据集上，FLASH生成的异常几乎消除与真实缺陷的校准差距，图像级F1达78.1%，接近真实异常上界83.6%，在各检测器间提供最一致的校准迁移，合成速度比逐样本生成方法快超过11.95倍。

Conclusion: FLASH通过解耦缺陷生成与合成，兼顾了流程化方法的高效性和生成式方法的多样性，仅依赖正常图像即可高效构建大规模工业异常数据集，性能接近真实缺陷水平。

Abstract: Synthetic anomaly generation helps expand industrial anomaly datasets when real defects are scarce or unavailable. Existing approaches lie at two extremes: procedural approaches are fast but struggle to represent complex anomalies, while generative approaches produce diverse defects but require costly per-sample generation. We present FLASH, a framework that decouples defect generation from anomaly synthesis under a ``generate once, synthesize many'' paradigm. Given only normal images, FLASH uses Vision-Language Model (VLM) guidance and an image-generation model to produce a small set of defect images, from which it extracts, validates, and banks reusable defect patches. For synthesis of anomalous images, Object Boundary Suppression (OBS) first identifies the probable foreground object-aware region of the host image, while Multi-Resolution Spectral Pyramid (MRSP) noise generates diverse, size-controllable masks that determine the defect location and spatial extent. It then composes a large and diverse synthetic anomalous image set by localizing the defect region, sampling size-controllable placement masks and seamlessly blending retrieved defects onto new defect-free images without further need for image generation. Experiments on the MVTec AD 2 dataset show that FLASH-generated anomalies nearly close the calibration gap on real defects, reaching 78.1% image-level F1 against an 83.6% real-anomaly upper bound and providing the most consistent calibration transfer across detectors among procedural and generative alternatives. Moreover, FLASH synthesizes anomalies more than 11.95x faster than per-sample generative approaches.

</details>


### [142] [What Comes Next? Omni-StoryBench for Evaluating Story-Grounded Omnimodal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37317)
*Sieun Hyeon,Yejoon Lee,Mintaek Lim,Woojin Kim,Jaeik Kim,Jaeyoung Do*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Omni-StoryBench，一个以故事为基底的 omnimodal 基准，评估模型能否在图像、旁白和语音上连贯地续写故事。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 omnimodal 评估多聚焦单一模态（文本、图像、语音）的独立生成质量，但各模态单独看似合理的输出未必能表达一致的共同事件，缺少系统级、跨模态连贯性的评测基准。

Method: 构建 900 个经严格验证的故事转换样本（源自开放许可儿童读物），每个实例给出当前故事页及结构化下一页条件，要求模型生成下一页插图、旁白和角色口语表达；提供真实下一页参考与语音元数据；采用模态专属指标，并结合以一致性为核心的 LLM-as-a-judge 评分标准，覆盖上下文保持、条件遵循、参考一致性与跨模态连贯性；在编排式、半编排式与原生 any-to-any 三类共 32 种基线配置上进行评测。

Result: 编排式（配合强视觉语言模型规划）表现最可靠；当前原生 omnimodal 模型常在输出完整性和可控性上表现不佳；文本侧性能与图像、语音质量相关，但图像生成与视觉连续性是被评估配置中最明显的瓶颈。

Conclusion: Omni-StoryBench 定位为系统级基准，用于衡量超越孤立模态质量的连贯 omnimodal 生成能力。

Abstract: Omnimodal evaluation should go beyond independent text, image, and speech production: individually plausible outputs may not express a coherent shared event. We introduce Omni-StoryBench, a story-grounded omnimodal benchmark evaluating whether models can coherently continue stories across image, narration, and speech. Each instance provides a current storybook page and structured next-page conditions, requiring models to generate the next illustration, narration, and spoken character utterance. Omni-StoryBench contains 900 rigorously validated story transitions from openly licensed children's books, with ground-truth next-page references and speech metadata. We evaluate systems with modality-specific metrics and consistency-centered LLM-as-a-judge rubrics for context preservation, condition following, reference consistency, and cross-modal coherence. Across 32 baseline configurations spanning orchestration, semi-orchestration, and native any-to-any paradigms, we find orchestration with strong VLM planning most reliable, while current native omnimodal models often struggle with output completeness and controllability. Our analysis shows text-side performance is associated with image and speech quality, but image generation and visual continuity form the clearest observed bottleneck among the evaluated configurations. These results position Omni-StoryBench as a system-level benchmark measuring coherent omnimodal generation beyond isolated modality quality.

</details>


### [143] [The Domain Is a Residue: Adapting Self-Supervised Features, Not Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37330)
*Thomas Deixelberger,Markus Steinberger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Representation Feature Adapter (RFA)，一个仅2.9M参数的适配器网络，通过在冻结的DINO特征空间上迁移"残差"实现去雾/雨/雪及渲染转照片，训练参数比CycleGAN-Turbo少约160倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去除雾、雨、雪或将渲染图转为照片，需要移除源域风格同时保留场景内容。非配对翻译方法依赖生成器直接看到源外观（像素、近似可逆隐变量或控制图）来保留场景。作者观察到DINO特征图能固定场景内容，而天气、光照和渲染风格仅作为13%-14%特征范数的残差存在，因此可以只迁移这部分残差。

Method: 提出 Representation Feature Adapter (RFA)，一个2.9M参数的网络，用于移动DINO特征中的风格残差。训练时仅训练适配器及其判别器，编码器和特征条件解码器（一次训练适用于所有条件）保持冻结。与此对比的基线包括CycleGAN-Turbo、REGEN、HyPER-GAN。

Result: 相比CycleGAN-Turbo，在雾上两项指标均领先，在夜间KID上领先，在雪、雨、雾霾上差异在噪声范围内。在sim-to-real上两项指标均超过REGEN和HyPER-GAN。只有RFA能在去雨时保留场景。去雨代价是场景结构损失：除雾外CycleGAN-Turbo在各条件下保留更多结构。在VAE隐变量上同一适配器退化为恒等映射，其他从未见过它的组的解码器仍能渲染其输出。

Conclusion: RFA通过仅适配DINO特征残差实现高效跨域图像翻译，可训练参数比CycleGAN-Turbo少约160倍，单条件训练时间不足其五分之一，并在多项任务上取得竞争性或更优表现。

Abstract: Clearing fog, rain or snow from footage, or turning renders into photographs, must remove the source domain and keep the scene. Unpaired translators carry it through because their generator sees the source appearance (pixels, a near-invertible latent or a control map) and keeps it. A DINO feature map fixes what is in the scene and carries weather, lighting and rendering style as a residue of 13 to 14% of the feature norm. We propose the Representation Feature Adapter (RFA), a 2.9M-parameter network that moves this residue. We train only the adapter and its discriminators; the encoder and a feature-conditioned decoder, trained once for all conditions, stay frozen. Against CycleGAN-Turbo it is ahead on both metrics on fog and on KID on night, and level within noise on snow, rain and haze. On sim-to-real it leads REGEN and HyPER-GAN on both metrics. Only the RFA removes the rain while keeping the scene. The removal costs scene structure: CycleGAN-Turbo keeps more on every condition but fog. On VAE latents the identical adapter collapses to the identity, and decoders from other groups that never saw it render its output. The RFA has about 160 times fewer trainable parameters than CycleGAN-Turbo and under a fifth of its per-condition training time.

</details>


### [144] [OFBD: Object-Focused Background Debiasing for Long-Tailed Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37331)
*Shenghan Chen,Yiming Liu,Zhipeng Deng,Haolin Wang,Jiale Zhou,Zhijian Wu,Xiankai Lu,Yafei Ou,Yefeng Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现长尾训练会导致背景偏置的表征与优化，尾类退化不仅源于样本不足，也源于无关背景特征的学习，据此提出从分布与优化两方面消除背景偏置的框架OFBD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长尾数据分布下的性能权衡是视觉识别的长期难题。现有方法多通过重平衡、表征学习或数据增强来提升尾类，但尾类退化的根本原因尚未被充分探究。

Method: 提出Object-Focused Background Debiasing (OFBD)框架，从分布和优化两个角度缓解背景偏置：一是Foreground-guided CutMix，在保留目标相关前景的同时多样化互补背景；二是Background-guided Feature Rectification，无需可学习参数或额外训练即可抑制背景偏置特征。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法提升了整体准确率，在尾类上取得显著增益，且可作为插件接入主流长尾方法，无需外部数据或预训练识别模型。

Conclusion: 尾类退化与背景偏置密切相关，从分布与优化两方面同时去偏可有效缓解该问题，OFBD具有通用性与即插即用特性。

Abstract: Balancing performance trade-offs on long-tailed data distributions remains a long-standing challenge in visual recognition. Existing methods mainly improve tail classes through re-balancing, representation learning, or data augmentation, but the underlying cause of tail class degradation is still insufficiently explored. In this paper, we find that standard long-tailed training induces background-biased representation and optimization: tail classes suffer larger background distribution shifts and become increasingly driven by background gradients. This reveals that tail degradation is not merely caused by insufficient samples, but also by the learning of irrelevant background features. To tackle this issue, we propose Object-Focused Background Debiasing (OFBD), a framework that mitigates background bias from both distribution and optimization perspectives. Specifically, Foreground-guided CutMix preserves target-related foregrounds while diversifying complementary backgrounds, and Background-guided Feature Rectification suppresses background-biased features without learnable parameters or additional training. Extensive experiments show that our method improves overall accuracy, achieves significant tail-class gains, and can serve as a plug-in for mainstream long-tailed methods without external data or pretrained recognition models. The code is available at: https://ofbd-neurips2026-longtail-learning.github.io/

</details>


### [145] [UGO: Unified Architecture for General Multi-Object Tracking by Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37339)
*Jer Pelhan,Alan Lukezic,Matej Kristan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UGO是一种统一的多目标跟踪器，结合示例条件检测头与实例传播头，并用无需训练的能量最小化整合方法生成像素级掩码和检测，在GMOT基准和视频目标计数上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用多目标跟踪（GMOT）需从单帧示例跟踪指定类别的所有实例。现有方法依赖边界框和代理训练，在非刚性物体、拥挤场景和干扰物上表现不佳，因此需要一种统一的开放类别跟踪方案。

Method: 提出UGO：在同一架构中结合预训练的示例条件检测头与实例传播头；使用无需训练的能量最小化整合方法，将重叠候选转化为互斥的像素级掩码和检测，解决过分割、重复和冲突；采用跨全局与实例层次的层级记忆及新的记忆管理协议。

Result: 在GMOT基准和视频目标计数任务上取得新的SOTA，并与专用MOT方法具有竞争力。

Conclusion: UGO为统一的开放类别多目标跟踪建立了强有力的范式。

Abstract: General multi-object tracking (GMOT) tracks all instances of a user-specified category from a single first-frame exemplar. Prior work relies on bounding boxes and surrogate training, and struggles with non-rigid objects, crowded scenes, and distractors. We introduce UGO, a unified GMOT tracker that pairs a pretrained exemplar-conditioned detection head with an instance-propagation head in a common architecture. A novel training-free, energy-minimization consolidation method converts overlapping proposals into exclusive pixel-wise masks and detections, resolving over-segmentation, duplicates, and conflicts. A hierarchical memory spanning global and instance levels improves recall and per-instance segmentation accuracy using a new memory management protocol. UGO sets a new state-of-the-art on GMOT benchmarks and video object counting, and is competitive with specialist MOT methods, establishing a strong paradigm for unified, open-category multi-object tracking.

</details>


### [146] [HyperSAM: A Promptable Foundation Model for Hyperspectral Remote Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37340)
*Li Pang,Xinqiao Wu,Jing Yao,Pedram Ghamisi,Jun Zhou,Zhengchao Chen,Deyu Meng,Xiangyong Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 HyperSAM，一个可提示的高光谱基础模型，通过数据合成管线与基于 SAM3 的谱段适配架构，缓解高光谱基础模型构建中的数据与预训练先验两难问题，在多种高光谱任务上展现强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高光谱遥感对地物级观测至关重要，但通用高光谱基础模型构建困难。主要瓶颈有二：一是大规模高光谱语料难以同时具备高空间分辨率与可靠稠密标注；二是许多高光谱模型几乎从零训练，未能充分利用现代视觉基础模型学到的几何与交互先验。

Method: 数据侧：HyperSAM 通过物理信息引导的丰度迁移生成器，从高分辨率 SpaceNet 多光谱影像合成全谱段高光谱立方体，并以 SAM3 生成的伪掩码提供以物体为中心的监督。模型侧：采用冻结的 SAM3 RGB 图像分支、由 RGB ViT 初始化的可训练高光谱侧编码器、ControlNet 风格零初始化特征注入，以及轻量级混合专家掩码精修器；同时引入 CromSS 风格的置信度选择，对噪声伪标签进行加权以提升训练鲁棒性。](

Result: 大量实验表明，HyperSAM 在多种高光谱任务上取得强泛化表现，包括分类、异常检测、变化检测、目标检测与机载溢油制图；并显示高质量合成高光谱数据可能比单纯扩大含噪高光谱监督更有效。

Conclusion: HyperSAM 通过数据合成与 SAM3 谱段适配的结合，为可提示高光谱基础模型提供了可行路径，证明高质量合成数据与复用视觉基础模型先验可有效提升跨任务泛化能力。

Abstract: Hyperspectral remote sensing provides dense spectral measurements that are indispensable for material-level Earth observation, yet the construction of a general-purpose hyperspectral foundation model remains difficult. Two bottlenecks are especially limiting. First, large hyperspectral corpora rarely provide high spatial resolution together with reliable dense annotations. Second, many hyperspectral models are still trained almost from scratch, so the geometric and interactive priors learned by modern vision foundation models are not fully reused. To alleviate these issues, we \highlight{present} \textbf{HyperSAM}, a promptable hyperspectral foundation model that couples a data-centric hyperspectral synthesis pipeline with a spectral adaptation architecture based on Segment Anything Model 3 (SAM3). On the data side, HyperSAM synthesizes full-spectrum hyperspectral cubes from high-resolution SpaceNet multispectral imagery through a physics-informed abundance-transfer generator, while SAM3-derived pseudo-masks provide object-centric supervision. On the model side, the latest implementation uses a frozen SAM3 RGB image branch, a trainable hyperspectral side encoder initialized from the RGB vision transformer (ViT), ControlNet-style zero-initialized feature injection, and a lightweight mixture-of-experts mask refiner. To enhance training robustness against noisy pseudo-labels, Cross-modal Sample Selection (CromSS)-style confidence selection is incorporated for noisy-label weighting. Extensive experiments show that HyperSAM obtains strong generalization on diverse hyperspectral tasks (e.g., classification, anomaly detection, change detection, target detection, and airborne oil-spill mapping) and that high-quality synthetic hyperspectral data can be more effective than simply scaling noisy hyperspectral supervision.

</details>


### [147] [When to Retrieve, When to Stay: Uncertainty-Aware Temporal Evidence Allocation for Streaming Video-LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37345)
*Xiang Hu,Jiazuo Yu,Lu Zhang,Yunzhi Zhuge,Huchuan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WRWS是一个免训练的框架，通过不确定性自适应地平衡时间新近性与查询相关性，为流式视频理解选择证据，从而提升Video-LLM的效率与准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频理解需要Video-LLM在因果约束下推理连续视觉流，但视觉历史增长时处理预算有限。仅选近期会遗漏相关历史证据，而仅用语义检索在相关性分数模糊时会挤掉有用的近期上下文，因此需要一种能平衡二者的证据选择方法。

Method: 提出WRWS（When to Retrieve, When to Stay）免训练框架：用轻量外部视觉-语言编码器对已观测历史打分查询相关性，自适应分配模块以相似度分布的归一化熵作为检索不确定性代理，在相关性线索可靠时偏向语义检索，在不确定时强化新近性先验。采用"先检索后编码"流程，先选证据再由目标Video-LLM编码。

Result: 在4个Video-LLM系列和多种模型规模上，于StreamingBench和OVO-Bench取得有竞争力的准确率；效率评估中，WRWS将平均视觉到回答时间降至SOTA方法的47.93%。

Conclusion: WRWS通过不确定性自适应的证据分配，兼顾时间新近性与查询相关性，在不训练的情况下提升了流式视频理解的准确率与处理效率，代码将开源。

Abstract: Streaming video understanding requires Video Large Language Models (Video-LLMs) to reason over continuous visual streams under causal constraints. As the visual history grows, a bounded visual?processing budget requires evidence selection that balances temporal recency with query relevance. Recent-only selection excludes potentially relevant historical evidence, whereas Semantic-only retrieval can displace useful recent context when relevance scores are ambiguous. We introduce WRWS (When to Retrieve, When to Stay), a training-free framework for uncertainty-adaptive evidence allocation. A lightweight external vision-language encoder scores query relevance across the observed history, while an adaptive allocation module uses the normalized entropy of the similarity distribution as a proxy for retrieval uncertainty. WRWS favors semantic retrieval when relevance cues are reliable and strengthens the recency prior under uncertainty. Following a retrieve-first, encode-later pipeline, WRWS selects evidence before target-model visual encoding, such that only the selected observations are processed by the costly target Video-LLM. Experiments across four Video-LLM families and multiple model scales demonstrate competitive accuracy on StreamingBench and OVO-Bench. In our efficiency evaluation, WRWS reduces average vision-to-answer time to 47.93% of the state-of-the-art method. Code will be released.

</details>


### [148] [TAEC: Trajectory-Aware Evidence Coordination for Multi-Step Visual RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37349)
*Yalun Wu,Bingzhou Wang,Boyang Wang,Peiying Wang,Shaojie He,Yunhan Wang,Shaozu Yuan,Jiawei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出免训练框架TAEC，通过在共享轨迹状态中追踪未解决的答案需求，协调多步视觉RAG中证据的选取、记忆保留和视觉细读层次，缓解"轨迹级证据利用退化"，在ViDoSeek、SlideVQA、MMLongBench-Doc上取得领先的免训练性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步视觉RAG虽反复检索视觉证据、更新中间状态并决定继续检索或作答，但检索到相关证据并不保证其在推理轨迹中被有效利用，存在"轨迹级证据利用退化"：冗余来源挤占上下文、已解决需求或无效检索的观察残留、视觉来源被以不足细节重访。

Method: 提出Trajectory-Aware Evidence Coordination (TAEC)，一个免训练框架，围绕未解决的答案需求协调证据使用。TAEC在共享轨迹状态中追踪这些需求，用以指导哪些证据进入上下文、累积记忆如何保留、以及视觉证据以何种细节层次被检查。

Result: 在ViDoSeek、SlideVQA和MMLongBench-Doc上采用统一评测协议，TAEC相对主流免训练视觉RAG基线取得最佳综合表现，并在多个专有视觉语言模型上取得最高平均准确率。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将证据与不断演化的推理需求对齐，能改善多步视觉RAG整个过程中的证据利用。

Abstract: Multi-step visual retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) answers complex questions by repeatedly retrieving visual evidence, updating an intermediate state, and deciding whether to continue searching or answer. Yet retrieving relevant evidence does not ensure its effective use throughout the reasoning trajectory. As multi-step reasoning progresses, redundant sources occupy context capacity needed for missing evidence, observations tied to resolved requirements or unproductive searches linger in context, and visual sources are revisited with insufficient detail for fine-grained reading. We term this loss of usable evidence over a reasoning trajectory trajectory-level evidence utilization degradation. To address it, we propose Trajectory-Aware Evidence Coordination (TAEC), a training-free framework that coordinates evidence use around unresolved answer requirements. TAEC tracks these requirements in a shared trajectory state to guide which evidence enters the context, how accumulated memory is retained, and at what level of detail visual evidence is examined. Under a unified evaluation protocol on ViDoSeek, SlideVQA, and MMLongBench-Doc, TAEC achieves the best overall performance against leading training-free visual RAG baselines, with the highest average accuracy across multiple proprietary vision-language models. These results demonstrate that aligning evidence with evolving reasoning needs improves evidence use throughout multi-step visual RAG.

</details>


### [149] [PCaPaint: Prostate Cancer Inpainting by Mitigating Shortcut Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37350)
*Levente Lippenszky,Hongxu Yang,Marcell Dömötör,Krisztian Koos,László Ruskó*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 PCaPaint，一种基于潜在扩散模型（LDM）的前列腺癌 MRI 合成肿瘤修补方法，通过高斯噪声填充条件图像、病灶区域误差加权训练目标以及多序列潜在表示设计，解决了捷径学习问题，并在下游分割、分类和检测任务中显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 系统在肿瘤特异性应用中受限于标注数据稀缺。合成肿瘤修补是缓解该问题的有前景方案，但前列腺癌 MRI 具有高分辨率、多序列的特点，带来挑战。现有基于 LDM 的方法虽能合成大体积肿瘤，却易陷入捷径学习——即简单复制由掩码病灶区域得到的条件图像，导致合成肿瘤纹理失真。

Method: 1）提出一种简单高效的条件策略：将条件图像用高斯噪声填充，并给出理论证明，以克服捷径学习；2）提出一种新的 LDM 训练目标，强调病灶区域内的预测误差；3）设计多序列潜在表示，将 T2w 扫描与 DWI&ADC 扫描分别用两个独立自编码器压缩，以保留各自不同的频率特征。

Result: 大量实验表明，生成的合成数据提升了前列腺病灶分割、患者级分类和病灶级检测的下游性能；与近期基于 LDM 的先进肿瘤修补方法相比，本方法在下游性能与合成图像质量上均显著更优。

Conclusion: PCaPaint 通过针对性设计有效缓解了 LDM 前列腺癌修补中的捷径学习问题，验证了高斯噪声条件填充、病灶误差加权目标及多序列潜在表示的有效性，为标注稀缺场景下的前列腺癌合成数据生成提供了可行且优越的方案。

Abstract: The development of AI systems for tumor-specific applications is limited by the scarcity of labeled data. Synthetic tumor inpainting offers a promising approach but faces challenges for prostate cancer MRI which contains high-resolution multi-sequence data. Although methods leveraging latent diffusion models (LDMs) enable large-volume synthesis, they are prone to shortcut learning, simply reproducing the condition image created by masking the lesion region. In this work, we introduce PCaPaint, a prostate cancer inpainting method based on LDMs that explicitly addresses this failure mode. To overcome shortcut learning that compromises synthetic tumor texture, we propose a simple yet efficient conditioning strategy in which the condition image is filled with Gaussian noise, and we provide theoretical justification. In addition, we propose a novel training objective for LDM that emphasizes the error within the lesion region. Furthermore, we introduce a multi-sequence latent design, in which T2w scans and DWI&ADC scans are compressed using two separate autoencoders to preserve their distinct frequency characteristics. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the generated synthetic data improves downstream performance in prostate lesion segmentation, patient-level classification and lesion-level detection. Furthermore, our method significantly outperforms a recent state-of-the-art LDM-based tumor inpainting method both in downstream performance and in synthetic image quality.

</details>


### [150] [Visual Anomaly Synthesis for Model Selection in Data Scarcity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37360)
*Daniel Pröll,Thomas Kraxner,Tobias Schaefer,Sebastian Hegenbart*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个无需目标资产缺陷样本、即可在无缺陷真实图像上合成严重程度分级缺陷的框架，用于缺陷检测模型的选型与验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业状态监测中的缺陷检测系统必须经过验证才可信，但缺陷样本稀少，对特定设备甚至完全不存在，导致模型选型与验证缺乏数据。

Method: 从文献中提炼常见失效模式的缺陷分类体系，转化为不同严重程度的规范性提示词；在无缺陷图像中裁剪感兴趣区域，用预训练图像生成模型（FLUX.2 [klein]）编辑；通过颜色匹配与混合提升与原图的结构一致性；再用评分器和估计的检测难度过滤生成样本。

Result: 在 MVTecAD 上的模型选择实验表明，相比凭借测试数据选出的最佳固定模型，图像 AUROC 的选型后悔值几乎减半；实验证明严重程度分级异常合成的必要性。水电站 Pelton 水轮机转轮原位监测案例中，基于 PatchCore 的异常检测模型在合成图像上选型与验证，取得较强检测性能（最优阈值下 94% 正确检测，AUROC 0.97），可可靠检测中度和严重缺陷，但早期缺陷仍具挑战性。

Conclusion: 合成严重程度分级缺陷可在无真实缺陷参考的情况下支撑模型选型与验证，并能有效检验检测器灵敏度，但早期缺陷检测仍是难点。

Abstract: Defect detection systems for industrial condition monitoring can only be relied upon if they are validated, yet defective samples are rare and, for a specific asset, often nonexistent. We present a framework that synthesizes severity-graded defects on real non-defective images without any defect references for the target asset, that can be used for model selection and validation. A defect taxonomy for common failure modes is distilled from literature into prescriptive prompts at varying defect severities. Regions of interest are cropped from in defect-free images and edited with a pre-trained image generation model ("FLUX.2 [klein]"). Color-matching and blending are employed to improve structural coherence with the original image. Generations are filtered out by a scorer and by estimated detection difficulty. Model selection experiments on MVTecAD show image AUROC choice regret over model selection can be nearly halved compared to the best fixed model chosen with access to test data. Experiments show the need for severity-graded anomaly synthesis. A case study investigates the proposed method for in-situ monitoring of Pelton turbine runners in hydropower, where real defect images are rare and expensive to collect. A PatchCorebased anomaly detection model is fit on Pelton turbine images and selected and validated using synthetic images, showing strong detection performance (94 % correct detection at optimal threshold and AUROC 0.97). The model reliably detects moderate and advanced defects, while early-stage defects remain challenging, indicating the synthetic data meaningfully stresses detector sensitivity.

</details>


### [151] [Think Before You Score: Thinking Reward Model for Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37372)
*Xuehai Bai,Zhenchen Tang,Yang Shi,Dianyi Wang,Tengfei Liu,Wanshun Su,Xuanyu Zhu,Ruohui Wang,Haiwen Diao,Haotian Wang,Xiaoling Gu,Yuanxing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"Think Before You Score"范式及Thinking Reward Model (TRM)，通过为每个案例自适应制定评估准则再进行打分，并引入PD-GRPO缓解分数极化问题，在图像生成/编辑奖励建模基准上达到开源SOTA，并有效提升生成模型的强化学习训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉奖励模型直接将任务条件和候选输出映射为标量奖励，未显式说明每个具体案例究竟应该评估什么，导致评估隐含且不具针对性。

Method: 提出TRM：先针对每个案例自适应构建评估准则（rubrics），再依据准则进行评估，输出细粒度的逐点奖励；同时发现传统成对偏好优化会导致分数极化，提出PD-GRPO，利用成对监督提升奖励区分度并保留细粒度逐点打分能力。

Result: 在图像生成与编辑的奖励建模基准上，TRM在开源奖励模型中达到SOTA，并可与专有模型高度竞争；作为强化学习奖励信号时，可一致性地提升多种视觉生成模型的表现。

Conclusion: 显式、案例自适应的细粒度奖励能转化为有效的优化信号，TRM在评估与生成优化两方面均展现价值。

Abstract: Visual reward models are essential for evaluating and improving visual generation models, yet existing approaches typically map task conditions and candidate outputs directly to scalar rewards, leaving implicit what should be evaluated for each individual case. We introduce Think Before You Score, a paradigm that explicitly determines what matters for each case before judging how well the candidate performs. Following this principle, we propose the Thinking Reward Model (TRM), which formulates case-adaptive rubrics, performs rubric-guided assessment, and produces fine-grained pointwise rewards. We further observe that conventional pairwise preference optimization can induce score polarization, and introduce Pairwise Dual-Group Relative Policy Optimization (PD-GRPO), which leverages pairwise supervision to improve reward discrimination while preserving fine-grained pointwise scoring. Extensive experiments on image generation and editing reward-modeling benchmarks demonstrate that TRM achieves state-of-the-art performance among open-source reward models while remaining highly competitive with proprietary alternatives. Moreover, using TRM as a reward for reinforcement learning consistently improves diverse visual generation models, demonstrating that its fine-grained, case-adaptive rewards translate into effective optimization signals for visual generation.

</details>


### [152] [MG-Thinker: Bi-Axial Self-Reflection for Multi-Image Reasoning Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37374)
*Heyu Huang,Chi Chen,Zonghao Guo,Yuhua Li,Maosong Sun,Ruixuan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MG-Thinker后训练强化学习框架，面向多图像推理定位（MRG）任务，构建25K数据集并设计Bi-Axial DAPO优势分解机制，实现精确像素级定位的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多图像推理定位（MRG）的强化学习方法忽略了该范式的两个内在特性：一是由粗到细的层次化推理模式，二是任务-样本难度的异质分布问题。

Method: 提出MG-Thinker后训练RL框架，并配以25K MRG数据集和任务自适应的CoT标注以激发多视角证据；同时提出Bi-Axial DAPO（BiA-DAPO），通过组内信号轴与组间能力轴两个互补机制分解rollout优势，二者均基于候选池以获得稳定的组级统计。

Result: 在多图像推理定位任务上达到最先进性能，并在多图像理解及多种多模态基准上持续提升泛化能力。

Conclusion: MG-Thinker通过层次化推理与异质难度自适应的优势分解，为多图像推理定位建立了新的有效范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has recently delivered substantial gains in multimodal reasoning, opening a promising route for fine-grained visual perception. Yet for multi-image reasoning grounding (MRG), reasoning over real-world multi-image contexts toward pixel-precise localization, existing RL-based approaches overlook two characteristics intrinsic to this paradigm: a coarse-to-fine hierarchical reasoning pattern, and heterogeneously distributed task--sample difficulties. In this work, we present MG-Thinker, a post-training RL framework that advances a new MRG paradigm featuring such hierarchical reasoning, supported by a curated 25K MRG dataset with task-adaptive Chain-of-Thought (CoT) annotations that elicit multi-perspective evidence before conclusion. To remedy the heterogeneous task--sample difficulties, we further propose Bi-Axial DAPO (BiA-DAPO), which decomposes rollout advantages along an intra-group signal axis and an inter-group competence axis through two complementary mechanisms, both grounded on our defined candidate pool for stable group-level statistics. Extensive experiments show that MG-Thinker achieves state-of-the-art performance on multi-image reasoning grounding while consistently improving generalization across multi-image understanding and diverse multimodal benchmarks.

</details>


### [153] [Do-JEPA: From Masking to Intervention in Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37378)
*Hossein Resani,Javen Qinfeng Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Do-JEPA,通过在模拟器中对比动作a与参考动作a∅下的潜在未来差异Δz来训练世界模型,使其区分"动作导致的变化"与"单纯共现的变化",在合成与像素实验中均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在世界模型只预测"下一步会发生什么",其目标函数无法区分动作导致的结果与仅与动作共现的结果。已有的对象掩码方法(如C-JEPA)只是干预预测器能看到什么,而非干预物理世界中真正发生了什么,因此无法从根本上建立动作与效果的因果联系。

Method: 从同一保存的模拟器状态出发,分别在动作a和参考动作a∅下运行动力学,训练模型预测两个潜在未来之差Δz=z^a−z^{a∅}。该目标(Do-JEPA)由四部分组成:效果损失、支持损失(动作进入的位置)、传播损失(效果扩散的路径)以及不变性损失(不应改变的部分)。

Result: 在对象对齐变量的合成系统中,支持监督在99.95%测试案例中找到被直接干预的对象,而稀疏动作掩码则全部将动作误送到无关槽位;响应起始监督可恢复环形传播图(边AUROC 0.975 vs 0.624)。在像素实验中,效果损失优于使用完全相同数据的对照组:端到端LeWM模型潜在效果误差降低28.4%,自然动作序列下物理效果误差降低13.5%;在三个独立生成的CausalWorld基准上,物理变化下响应效果误差降低约20%,预测效果的潜在上下文敏感性降低66%。从零训练会损失事实准确性,但对已有模型微调可消除此代价。

Conclusion: 通过干预世界本身而非模型可见内容,可帮助潜在世界模型更准确地预测其动作所导致的结果,验证了因果干预式目标在提升世界模型效果理解方面的有效性。

Abstract: Latent world models are trained to predict what happens next, so nothing in their objective separates what an action caused from what merely co-occurred with it. Object-masking models such as C-JEPA intervene on what the predictor can see; we intervene on what physically happens. From one saved simulator state we run the dynamics under an action $a$ and under a reference action $a_{\varnothing}$, and train the model to predict the difference $Δz=z^{a}-z^{a_{\varnothing}}$ between the two latent futures. The resulting objective, Do-JEPA, has an effect loss, a support loss (where the action enters), a propagation loss (where its effect travels) and invariance losses (what must not change). In a synthetic system with object-aligned variables, support supervision finds the directly intervened object in 99.95% of test cases, where a sparse action mask sends the action to a nuisance slot in every case, and response-onset supervision recovers the ring-shaped propagation graph (edge AUROC 0.975 vs. 0.624). From pixels, the effect loss beats a control trained on exactly the same data: it lowers latent effect error by 28.4% on an end-to-end LeWM model and physical effect error by 13.5% when trained and tested on natural action sequences, and on three independently generated CausalWorld benchmarks it lowers responsive effect error by about 20% under physics shifts and the latent context sensitivity of predicted effects by 66%. Trained from scratch it costs factual accuracy; fine-tuning an existing model with it removes this cost. Together, these results show that intervening on the world, rather than on what the model sees, helps latent world models predict what their actions cause.

</details>


### [154] [Anatomy-Aware Prediction of Bronchoscopic Accessibility from 3D CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37386)
*Linkai Peng,Cuiling Sun,Bin Wang,Jamie Rowell,Catherine Gao,Oyku Ikizgul,Eminenur Sentasci,Andrea Bejar,Halil Ertugrul Aktas,Gorkem Durak,Momen Wahidi,Christopher Kapp,Ulas Bagci*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次将支气管镜可达性预测形式化为监督学习任务，提出解剖感知混合专家（MoE）端到端框架，并构建首个438例临床数据集，AUROC达0.8052，超越现有方法与人类专家。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 支气管镜检查的术前规划对肺部病灶诊断至关重要，而当前的可达性评估依赖对CT扫描的主观人工判读，既耗时又存在观察者间差异，亟需自动化、客观的预测方法。

Method: 提出解剖感知混合专家（MoE）模型，集成三个专门模块：CT专家（提取局部形态特征）、肺叶专家（引入解剖先验）、路径几何专家（编码支气管树的序列约束）；并构建首个包含438例术前CT与手术结局记录的临床数据集。

Result: 实验结果显示，该方法AUROC达到0.8052，显著优于当前最优基线方法和经验丰富的人类专家。

Conclusion: 该工作为肺医学中的计算机辅助介入规划建立了新的基准，数据与代码将公开于 https://nubagcilab.github.io/BronchoAccess/ 。

Abstract: Pre-operative planning for bronchoscopy is critical for the diagnosis of lung lesions. Current accessibility assessment relies on subjective manual inspection of CT scans, which is time-consuming and prone to inter-observer variability. In this paper, we formalize bronchoscopy accessibility prediction as a novel supervised learning task and present the first end-to-end framework to address it. We propose an Anatomy-Aware Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) model that integrates specialized modules: a CT Expert for local morphological features, a Lobe Expert for anatomical priors, and a Path Geometry Expert that encodes the sequential constraints of the bronchial tree. To support this task, we curated the first clinical dataset of 438 cases with pre-operative CT scans and documented procedural outcomes. Experimental results demonstrate that our method achieves an AUROC of 0.8052, significantly outperforming both state-of-the-art baselines and experienced human experts. This work establishes a new benchmark for computer-aided interventional planning in pulmonary medicine. Our data and code will be publicly available at https://nubagcilab.github.io/BronchoAccess/.

</details>


### [155] [Multi-task learning for the automatic grading of enlarged perivascular space burden using MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37387)
*Jesse Phitidis,William N. Whiteley,Joanna M. Wardlaw,Miguel O. Bernabeu,Yajun Cheng,Xiaodi Liu,Junfang Zhang,Una Clancy,Stephen Makin,Roberto Duarte Coello,Susana Muñoz Maniega,Mark E. Bastin,Simon R. Cox,Maria del C. Valdés Hernández*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出并评估了多种机器学习方法，利用半自动生成的"银标准"PVS分割掩膜和放射学评分，自动评估基底节与半卵圆中心的血管周围间隙（PVS）负担，其中多任务CNN表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑MRI中扩大的血管周围间隙（PVS）与脑健康不良相关，但放射科医生依据临床分级量表进行视觉评分耗时且存在观察者间差异，因此需要自动化方法以加速分析并消除主观差异。

Method: 基于Potters/Wardlaw量表，研究创新性地在训练中同时使用不完美的半自动"银标准"PVS分割掩膜和PVS放射学评分，并对三种方法进行比较评估：接受PVS掩膜作为额外输入通道的条件CNN、同时进行PVS分割与评分的多任务CNN，以及利用PVS掩膜特征的逻辑回归模型。

Result: 多任务学习最为有效，平均精度（mAP）达64.08%，优于条件CNN（60.22%）、仅预测PVS评分的基线CNN（52.11%）和逻辑回归模型（49.32%）；多任务模型还能定位单个PVS，并以概率合理的方式在较难类别上给出较低置信度。

Conclusion: 多任务CNN是自动评估PVS负担的最佳方法，其预测与年龄、性别、高血压、白质高信号体积及缺血性卒中病灶状态等临床因素的真实关联一致，表明该模型具有临床合理性。

Abstract: Enlarged perivascular spaces (PVS) visible in brain magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) are increasingly thought to be linked to poor brain health. PVS are elongated structures of less than 3 mm in diameter and can be numerous. To reflect the incidence of PVS, radiologists visually score their burden following a clinical grading scale - a task that would benefit from automation to accelerate analyses and overcome the influence of inter-observer differences. We developed and evaluated methods for training machine learning models to score PVS incidence in the basal ganglia (BG) and centrum semiovale (CSO) leveraging the Potters/Wardlaw scale. The novelty in our work lies in the use of imperfect, semi-automatically generated "silver-standard" PVS segmentation masks during training, in addition to PVS radiological scores. We comparatively evaluated a conditional convolutional neural network (CNN) which accepts PVS masks as an extra input channel, a multi-task CNN which performs both PVS segmentation and scoring, and a logistic regression model which utilises features derived from PVS masks to predict PVS scores. Multi-task learning was the most effective method, achieving a mean average precision of 64.08% compared to 60.22% for the conditional CNN, 52.11% for a baseline CNN trained only to predict PVS scores, and 49.32% for the logistic regression model. The multi-task model showed an ability to localise individual PVS not shown by the other CNNs, and behaved in a probabilistically sensible way, predicting with lower confidence on inherently harder classes. Age, sex, hypertension status, white matter hyperintensity volume, and ischaemic stroke lesion status were shown to be associated with the multi-task model's PVS score predictions and the ground truth in a similar way.

</details>


### [156] [BeatDance: Generating Beat-Consistent 3D Dance with Hierarchical Spatial-Temporal Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37400)
*Xiaojian Shen,Dahu Shi,Jianrong Zhang,Hai Li,Hongwei Zhao,Dawei Zhang,Yunzhi Zhuge,Zhiliang Wu,Guanghui Yue,Wei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于扩散模型的BeatDance框架，通过层次化解耦注意力和循环一致性学习，实现音乐到3D舞蹈生成中与节拍的精准对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从音乐生成逼真3D舞蹈需在捕捉人体运动空间复杂性的同时与音乐节奏精确同步。现有方法虽能生成物理上合理的舞蹈动作，但往往难以实现与音乐（如节拍）的精确对齐。

Method: 提出扩散框架BeatDance，含两组件：1）分层解耦注意力（HDA）模块，先解耦人体姿态与时间动态的学习，再用分层结构捕捉短期和长期依赖，增强时空建模；2）采用循环一致性学习，引入辅助的舞蹈到音乐模块，训练时重构音乐与原音乐的差异产生更强损失信号，促进音乐与舞蹈运动的一致性。

Result: 在两个基准数据集上，所提方法优于近期竞争性方法。

Conclusion: BeatDance通过分层解耦注意力和循环一致性学习有效提升音乐-舞蹈的节拍对齐与时空建模能力，生成效果优于现有方法。

Abstract: Generating realistic 3D dance from music is a challenging task that requires accurate synchronization with musical rhythms while capturing the spatial complexity of human motion. Although existing methods can generate physically plausible dance motions, they often struggle to achieve precise alignment with music, such as the beat. To address this limitation, we propose a novel diffusion-based framework, BeatDance, with two components: 1) We present a Hierarchical Decoupled Attention (HDA) module, which first disentangles the learning of human pose and temporal dynamics. A hierarchical structure is then employed to capture both short-term and long-term dependencies, thereby enhancing spatial-temporal modeling. 2) We adopt cycle-consistent learning by introducing an auxiliary dance-to-music module. During training, discrepancies between the reconstructed and original music induce a stronger loss signal, effectively encouraging the consistency property between the music and dance motion. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that our proposed approach outperforms recent competitive methods on two benchmark datasets.

</details>


### [157] [Complementary Retrieval-Augmented Prompting for Consistent Long-Form Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37407)
*Xianghan Wei,Xiaoda Yang,Zhi Wang,An Pan,Daoan Zhang,Huayi Zhang,Yan Zhang,Wei Xu,Zishun Liao,Jianwen Lou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需训练的智能体框架（互补检索增强提示），通过聚合互补的历史参考帧，为长视频生成提供完整且有针对性的条件控制，提升跨镜头一致性与文本可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频生成的核心瓶颈在于独立生成各镜头时难以保持角色、场景、物体的一致性。现有免训练方法依赖检索历史画面作为参考，但这些参考常存在严重的信息不匹配：要么引入无关冗余上下文，要么无法提供目标镜头所需的完整元素组合。

Method: 提出互补检索增强提示（Complementary Retrieval-Augmented Prompting）框架：1）将叙事脚本解析为基于文本的视觉元素登记表，追踪角色、物体、场景及其镜头级状态；2）用VLM标注的关键帧库将元素映射到过去的视觉观测；3）智能体在所需元素引导下检索互补参考，最大化目标元素覆盖并最小化历史噪声；4）将检索到的参考、结构化元素状态与 grounding 指令组装为统一提示，输入冻结的视频生成器。整个过程无需重训练或修改底层生成器，且完全可解释。

Result: 在多镜头故事生成的定量与定性评估中，该方法在跨镜头一致性和文本可控性上持续优于 recent-frame、memory-based 和 entity-level 检索基线。

Conclusion: 通过元素感知的互补检索与结构化提示组装，可在不训练的情况下为长视频生成提供全面且可解释的条件控制，显著改善跨镜头一致性与文本可控性。 

Abstract: While recent video foundation models excel at generating high-quality short videos, long-form video generation remains a critical challenge, where a major bottleneck lies in conditioning independently generated shots to preserve consistent characters, scenes, and objects throughout a story. Existing training-free approaches typically condition target shots using retrieved historical visuals. However, these references often suffer from severe informational mismatch, either introducing irrelevant contextual redundancy or failing to provide the full combination of required elements for the target shot. To resolve this, we present Complementary Retrieval-Augmented Prompting, an agentic framework that strategically aggregates a compact set of mutually supportive historical references to achieve complete and targeted conditioning for long-form video generation without retraining or modifying the underlying generator. Specifically, our framework explicitly models the visual elements required by each target shot by parsing the narrative script into a text-grounded visual element registry that tracks characters, objects, scenes, and their shot-level states. A VLM-annotated keyframe library further maps these elements to past visual observations. Guided by the required elements, our agent retrieves complementary references that maximize target-element coverage while minimizing historical noise. Finally, the retrieved references, structured element states, and grounding instructions are assembled into a unified prompt for the frozen video generator. This element-aware process provides comprehensive conditioning while remaining fully interpretable. Quantitative and qualitative evaluations on multi-shot story generation demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms recent-frame, memory-based, and entity-level retrieval baselines in cross-shot consistency and text-controllability.

</details>


### [158] [LazySloth: Bounded LLM-based Lazy Tree Search for Fast Long Video Comprehension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37426)
*Arka Mukherjee,Kaleen Shrestha,Larissa Zhu,Maja Matarić*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LazySloth 是一种基于树的搜索方法，通过只对 VLM 判定为无关的视频片段进行有界描述，加速长视频理解与检索 2.9-8.3 倍，并在多个基准上保持相近或更优的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长视频理解方法多依赖对抽取帧进行粗粒度描述，计算效率低且需要大上下文窗口；基于多模态 RAG 的高效方法则使用有损嵌入，丢失时序上下文和细粒度细节。目前少有研究探讨如何优化基于 VLM 的查询相关信息检索。

Method: 提出 LazySloth，一种高效的树状搜索方法。它对视频中被 VLM 判定为无关的部分仅做有界（受限）描述，从而减少描述开销，加速视频理解与检索任务。

Result: 相比现有 agentic 方法提速 2.9-8.3 倍；在 Gemma 4 31B 和 Qwen3.6 27B 两个开源基座 VLM、四个基准上达到相近或更优的任务准确率，并缩小了与闭源模型 GPT-4o 的差距。消融实验显示：用 CLIP 检索替代 VLM 场景理解会损失 8.8-19.9% 准确率；惰性树构建以极小的描述成本达到与急切构建相当的效果。

Conclusion: LazySloth 证明可在不显著损失性能的前提下实现更快的长视频理解。

Abstract: Modern vision-language models (VLMs) have shown promising results in long-video understanding due to the rich semantic information they can capture. However, most methods focus on coarse captioning of extracted image frames that are computationally inefficient and require models with large context windows. While past work has explored efficient methods through multimodal retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), they rely on lossy embeddings that lose temporal context and fine-grained detail. Few works to date have investigated how VLM-based query-relevant information retrieval can be optimized. We introduce LazySloth, an efficient tree-based search method that speeds up video comprehension and retrieval tasks 2.9-8.3x (compared to existing agentic methods) through bounded captioning of portions of the video considered irrelevant by a VLM of the video. Compared to contemporary specialized video-understanding VLMs and RAG-based methods, LazySloth achieved similar or better final task accuracy across two recent open-source base VLMs--Gemma 4 31B and Qwen3.6 27B--across four benchmarks. LazySloth reduced the gap between the base open-source model and a closed-source model, GPT-4o. Ablations showed that replacing VLM scene understanding with CLIP-based retrieval cost 8.8-19.9% in accuracy, while lazy tree construction matches eager construction at a fraction of the captioning cost. With LazySloth, we demonstrate the possibility of faster long-video comprehension without substantial loss in performance.

</details>


### [159] [Event-Only Wingbeat Counting under Camera Motion: A Controlled MuJoCo Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37465)
*Zhang Nengbo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个受控MuJoCo基准，用于在事件相机观测中计数完整振翅周期，将运动训练与仅事件的图像平移补偿分离，并通过2×2消融比较神经模型与多种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完成振翅的计数需要识别单个周期，而非仅估计主导频率，这在频率变化和停顿时尤为困难；同时相机运动会在事件观测中把目标与背景的亮度变化混合在一起，干扰计数。

Method: 构建受控MuJoCo基准：324条流，来自24个独立场景、3种拍动几何、2个距离（1.5与3.0 m）以及静止、中等运动、较强运动三种视角；15场景拟合、3场景验证、6场景留出测试。使用固定的因果时序卷积网络，在配对2×2消融中进行三组初始化种子评估，并与ridge、Fourier、自相关及改编的EEPPR基线对比。

Result: 中等运动下，配对运动训练将计数平均绝对误差从31.130降至3.185周期（1.5 m），从42.019降至5.444（3.0 m）；加入所测试的补偿后误差升至4.630和10.185。Fourier基线在1.5 m中等运动下达到0.944周期，说明神经模型并非普遍最优。同时报告精确计数准确率和时间匹配的周期F1。

Conclusion: 结果支持在该小型合成基准中采用运动感知训练，同时揭示简单事件-背景稳定化的局限；这些结论不能确立真实传感器性能、气动飞行表现或对未见飞行器类型的泛化能力。

Abstract: Counting completed wingbeats requires identifying individual cycles, including during frequency changes and pauses; estimating a dominant frequency alone is insufficient. Camera motion further mixes target and background brightness changes in event observations. We present a controlled MuJoCo benchmark that separates motion training from event-only image translation compensation. The acquisition contains 324 streams from 24 independent scenes, three flapping geometries, two distances (1.5 and 3.0 m), and static, moderate-motion and stronger-motion views. Fifteen scenes are used for fitting, three for validation and six for held-out testing. A fixed causal temporal convolutional network is evaluated in a matched 2 x 2 ablation with three initialization seeds and compared with ridge, Fourier, autocorrelation and an adapted EEPPR baseline. Under moderate motion, paired motion training reduces count mean absolute error from 31.130 to 3.185 cycles at 1.5 m and from 42.019 to 5.444 at 3.0 m. Adding the tested compensation increases these errors to 4.630 and 10.185, respectively. A Fourier baseline achieves 0.944 cycles at 1.5 m under moderate motion, showing that the neural model is not uniformly best. We report exact-count accuracy and temporally matched cycle F1 alongside count error. These findings support motion-aware training in this small synthetic benchmark, while exposing limits of simple event-background stabilization. They do not establish real-sensor performance, aerodynamic flight, or generalization to unseen vehicle types.

</details>


### [160] [Label Less, Learn More: Resource-Efficient Active Semi-Supervised Learning for Onboard Satellite Image Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37481)
*Ahmed Abdelnaby,Mohamed Elmahallawy,Marius Bernahrndt,Tobias Hecking*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SatLabel是一个资源感知学习框架，通过自适应样本采集与半监督模型适配，将有限的卫星标注转化为逐步精炼的星上模型，在11个遥感数据集上以约11.2M参数实现了优于RemoteCLIP零样本推理的Macro-F1表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模普适感知依赖高分辨率卫星影像，但星上视觉任务受限于昂贵的标注成本以及有限的计算、内存、能量和通信资源。现有方法多依赖数据饥渴的监督学习或大型视觉-语言基础模型，难以在这些约束下高效适配与部署。

Method: 提出SatLabel框架：不再对均匀采样的标签反复训练，而是构建模型不确定性、类别不平衡与伪标签质量之间的闭环，选择性采集信息量大的样本，并利用大量无标注影像，使紧凑的学生模型适应目标感知域，降低标注与推理成本。此外引入可选的混合专家（MoE）学生模型，结合基于图的特征精炼，在保持轻量化的同时增强表征能力。

Result: 在覆盖核心、扩展和未见域的11个遥感数据集上，与RemoteCLIP零样本推理对比，SatLabel在多数核心与扩展数据集上提升了Macro-F1，并保持较强的跨数据集迁移能力。Balanced学生模型仅含11.2M参数、约42.8MB，而RemoteCLIP为151.3M参数、577MB；计算量为3.65 versus 5.89 GFLOPs。在四个效率基准上，GPU前向吞吐量约提升2倍，并降低了单张影像能耗。

Conclusion: SatLabel通过自适应样本采集与半监督适配，在显著降低参数、存储、计算与能耗的同时提升了遥感感知性能，为资源受限的星上视觉部署提供了高效可行的方案。

Abstract: Large-scale pervasive sensing increasingly relies on high-resolution satellite imagery, yet task-specific onboard vision is constrained by costly annotation and limited computation, memory, energy, and communication resources. Existing approaches largely rely on either data-hungry supervised learning or large vision-language foundation models, limiting efficient adaptation and deployment under these constraints. We present SatLabel, a resource-aware learning framework that transforms limited satellite labels into progressively refined onboard models through adaptive sample acquisition and semi-supervised model adaptation. Rather than repeatedly training on uniformly sampled labels, SatLabel closes the loop between model uncertainty, class imbalance, and pseudo-label quality to selectively acquire informative samples while exploiting abundant unlabeled imagery. This enables a compact student to adapt to target sensing domains with reduced annotation and inference costs. We further introduce an optional Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) student with graph-based feature refinement to enhance representation capacity while retaining a lightweight footprint. We evaluate SatLabel on 11 remote-sensing datasets spanning core, extended, and unseen domains against RemoteCLIP zero-shot inference. SatLabel improves Macro-F1 on most core and extended datasets while maintaining strong cross-dataset transfer to unseen domains. More importantly, the Balanced student contains only 11.2 M parameters and occupies approximately 42.8 MB, compared with 151.3M parameters and 577 MB for RemoteCLIP, while requiring 3.65 versus 5.89 GFLOPs. Across four efficiency benchmarks, it achieves approximately 2x higher GPU-forward throughput and reduces energy per image on datasets.

</details>


### [161] [PoE-Fuse: Precision-Weighted Expert Fusion for Bi-Temporal Change Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37485)
*Haruki Watase,Shunya Nagashima,Takayuki Nishimura*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PoE-Fuse，一种参数高效框架，通过组合冻结的几何、定位与语言基础专家并仅训练轻量融合主干，统一解决双时相变化检测、建筑物定位与损毁评估任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双时相变化理解对灾害响应和环境监测至关重要，但强视觉语言模型适配通常需要全量微调或强化学习，成本高且不稳定。

Method: PoE-Fuse组合冻结的基础专家，将其特征重采样到共享空间网格；把对齐特征视为潜在场景状态的高斯观测，按学习的逐单元精度进行融合。该专家乘积估计器严格推广了均匀求和与标量门控，并通过组合两个时间戳的精度扩展到变化场。

Result: 单一共享主干同时解决三个任务，平均F1达59.2%，而指令微调的时序视觉语言助手为40.7%，并超过在相同协议和训练预算下重训练的专用变化检测模型。

Conclusion: PoE-Fuse以参数高效方式实现多任务双时相变化理解，性能优于基线，验证了专家乘积融合的有效性。

Abstract: Bi-temporal change understanding, which localizes and characterizes what changed between two satellite images, is central to disaster response and environmental monitoring, spanning change detection, building localization, and damage assessment. Strong vision-language models address these tasks, but adapting them typically requires full fine-tuning or reinforcement learning, which is costly and unstable. We propose PoE-Fuse, a parameter-efficient framework that instead composes frozen foundation experts for geometry, grounding, and language, resampling their features onto a shared spatial grid and training only a lightweight fusion trunk. PoE-Fuse treats the aligned features as Gaussian observations of a latent scene state and fuses them by learned per-cell precision. This product-of-experts estimator strictly generalizes uniform summation and scalar gating, and extends to change fields by composing the precisions of the two timestamps. A single shared trunk solves the three tasks at once, reaching a mean F1 of 59.2%, compared with 40.7% for an instruction-tuned temporal vision-language assistant, and surpassing dedicated change-detection models retrained under the same protocol and training budget.

</details>


### [162] [Physics-Guided Flow-Map Matching for Precipitation Nowcasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37487)
*Shunya Nagashima,Takumi Bannai,Makoto Misaizu,Keisuke Maeda,Takahiro Ogawa,Miki Haseyama*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PG-FMM，一种解耦可预测平流与不确定小尺度细节的条件流映射模型，用冻结的拉格朗日平流先验引导流映射生成头，四步采样生成清晰随机细节。在四个雷达基准上24项指标中18项超越SOTA，强降雨阈值下CSI最高提升58.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降水临近预报对洪水预警和灾害响应至关重要，但混沌动力学、重尾强度和稀有高强度结构使其成为时空生成建模的严苛基准。确定性模型因最小化像素损失而趋向条件均值，模糊了最关键的高强度结构；在确定性骨干上叠加随机残差的生成模型也继承了同样的模糊问题。

Method: 提出物理引导流映射匹配（PG-FMM），一种条件流映射模型，将可预测的平流与不确定的小尺度细节解耦。使用冻结的拉格朗日平流先验输送雷达场并提供显式运动预报；流映射生成头以过去帧和先验推演为条件（而非简单相加），在四步采样中产生清晰的随机细节。先验仅作引导，使生成头替换模糊结构而非继承它。

Result: 在四个雷达基准上的大量实验表明，PG-FMM在24项指标中有18项优于当前最先进方法，在强降雨阈值下增益最大，临界成功指数（CSI）提升最高达58.9%。

Conclusion: PG-FMM通过解耦平流与细节、以物理先验引导流映射生成，有效解决了传统模型模糊高强度结构的核心痛点，在强降雨等关键场景显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Precipitation nowcasting, generating future radar fields from past observations, is critical for flood warning and disaster response. It is also a demanding benchmark for spatiotemporal generative modeling, with chaotic dynamics, heavy-tailed intensities, and rare high-intensity structures that matter most. Deterministic models minimize a pixel loss and are driven toward the conditional mean, which blurs exactly those structures, while generative models that add a stochastic residual on top of a deterministic backbone inherit the same blur. We propose Physics-Guided Flow-Map Matching (PG-FMM), a conditional flow-map model that decouples predictable advection from uncertain small-scale detail. A frozen Lagrangian advection prior transports the radar field and supplies an explicit motion forecast, and a flow-map generative head, conditioned on the past frames and the prior rollout rather than summed onto it, produces sharp stochastic detail in four sampling steps. The prior serves only as guidance, so the head replaces blurred structure instead of inheriting it. Extensive experiments on four radar benchmarks show that PG-FMM outperforms state-of-the-art methods on 18 of 24 metrics, with the largest gains at heavy-rain thresholds, where the critical success index improves by up to 58.9%. The project page can be found at https://neurogica.github.io/PG-FMM.

</details>


### [163] [FORUM: Frozen Outputs Reconciled Using Model Agreement for Visual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37488)
*Taiyo Sato,Takamasa Sanda,Keisuke Maeda,Takahiro Ogawa,Miki Haseyama,Shunya Nagashima*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FORUM，一种无需训练的测试时融合方法，通过“一致性选择”和“中心点定位”两条几何规则整合多个冻结多模态大模型，在对抗性指代表达理解基准上显著超越大规模参考模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结的多模态大模型在标准指代表达理解任务上表现良好，但在含同类干扰物和否定的对抗基准上，即使最大模型也会自信出错，且重采样无法纠正。由于不同数据与架构训练的模型很少被同一混淆因素误导，其一致性可作为无标签的正确目标信号。

Method: FORUM采用无需训练的测试时融合策略，遵循两条固定几何规则：（1）一致性选择，保留被最多不同模型支持的区域；（2）中心点定位，返回实际成员框而非坐标平均，避免单个宽松预测偏移结果。

Result: 融合三个开源多模态大模型后，FORUM在对抗性Ref-Adv-s基准上平均准确率相对397B参数参考模型提升5%，比简单平均集成提升15%；增益可迁移至标准RefCOCO+；在无主导成员的均衡组合下仍超越397B模型5%。

Conclusion: 通过一致性选择与中心点定位的测试时融合，可在不训练的前提下有效提升多模态大模型在对抗性与标准指代表达理解任务上的鲁棒性和准确率。

Abstract: Frozen multimodal large language models (MLLMs) now solve standard referring expression comprehension with a single prompted call, yet on adversarial benchmarks with same-category distractors and negation, even the largest models are confidently wrong, and resampling repeats the error. Models built from different data and architectures rarely fall for the same confounder, so their agreement is a strong label-free signal of the correct target. We present FORUM, a training-free test-time fusion of frozen MLLMs guided by two fixed geometric rules: agreement-based selection keeps the region supported by the most distinct models, and medoid localization returns an actual member box instead of a coordinate average, so one loose prediction cannot shift the answer. Fusing three open MLLMs, FORUM surpasses the 397B-parameter published reference by a relative 5% in mean accuracy on the adversarial Ref-Adv-s benchmark, and a plain averaging ensemble by 15%. The gains transfer to standard RefCOCO+, and a balanced lineup with no dominant member still surpasses the 397B model by 5%.

</details>


### [164] [Attention-Scoped Guidance: Training-Free Spatial Control for Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37492)
*Zeyan Li,Wei Zhou,Hadi Amirpour,Minghao Zou,Panqi Yang,Jianfeng Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Attention-Scoped Guidance (ASG)，一种无需训练、无需外部掩码的采样器包装方法，将双分类器无引导中的全局权重变为空间自适应权重，从而在指令图像编辑中提升未编辑区域的保持效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令引导的图像编辑应只改变指令所指内容，其余部分保持不变。但在双 CFG 编辑中，文本引导与源图锚定的权重在每个去噪步骤都是全局统一的，导致编辑区域外也受到修改影响，破坏了保持性。

Method: ASG 从编辑器已计算的指令注意力中读取一个软支持图（soft support map），据此在支持度低处减弱文本引导，在支持度高处理增强图像锚定，使权重具有空间性。该包装器不需要训练、不需要外部掩码，也不增加额外的网络前向计算。

Result: 在完整的 MagicBrush 和 PIE-Bench++ 数据集上，ASG 提升了以保持性为导向的指标：在 MagicBrush 四项指标中领先三项，并提升 PIE-Bench++ 的背景 PSNR。一个剂量匹配的对照实验（去除空间分布）在 PIE-Bench++ 上最多损失 0.73 CLIP，证明增益来自空间分配本身。

Conclusion: 将引导权重进行空间化分配能够以零训练成本改善编辑的保持性，且该增益确实来源于空间分布方式，而非引导强度总量。

Abstract: Instruction-guided image editing should change what the instruction names and leave the rest of the image untouched. In dual classifier-free guidance (CFG), an editor combines two directions at every denoising step, one that pushes toward the instructed edit and one that pulls back toward the source image, using global weights. We introduce Attention-Scoped Guidance (ASG), a sampler wrapper that makes these weights spatial. It reads a soft support map from the instruction attention that the editor already computes, then weakens text guidance where support is low and strengthens image anchoring where support is high. The wrapper requires no training, no external mask, and no additional network evaluation. On the full MagicBrush and PIE-Bench++ splits, ASG improves preservation-oriented metrics, leading three of four MagicBrush metrics and PIE-Bench++ background PSNR. A dose-matched control that removes the spatial placement loses up to 0.73 CLIP on PIE-Bench++, confirming that the spatial allocation itself carries the gain.

</details>


### [165] [MotionMaestro: Masked Tokenization for Unified Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37495)
*Yun Chen,Munchurl Kim,Jeonghyeok Do*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一运动生成框架 MotionMaestro，通过掩码运动分词学习共享表征，支持文本、姿态、轨迹等多种条件任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动生成方法多针对单一任务（如文本生成运动、姿态条件生成、轨迹控制），缺乏能统一处理不同条件的框架，系统复杂。作者观察到各种运动条件可自然视为对运动序列的不同观测模式，即不同的掩码策略。

Method: MotionMaestro 通过掩码运动分词学习完整运动与异质部分观测的共享表征。采用三阶段训练：先学习掩码运动分词器，再在干净运动上精炼重建能力，最后在学到的隐空间中训练条件流匹配生成器。同时引入观测图和观测损失，在生成过程中显式保留给定的运动条件。

Result: 在 RoMo 和 MotionMillion 大规模数据集上，于多种运动生成任务中取得最先进性能。

Conclusion: 统一表征与条件机制使 MotionMaestro 支持文本引导与无条件合成、姿态条件与部分补全、时序插值、轨迹控制及运动延续等多种任务，验证了统一框架的可行性与优越性。

Abstract: Human motion generation plays an important role in applications such as character animation, virtual environments, and embodied interaction. While existing approaches have achieved remarkable progress, many of them are developed for individual tasks, including text-to-motion, pose-conditioned generation, and trajectory control. Although these tasks involve different types of conditions, a unified framework capable of handling them within a common representation would greatly simplify motion generation systems. We observe that diverse motion conditions can be naturally formulated as different observation patterns over motion sequences, where each task corresponds to a specific masking strategy. Based on this insight, we introduce MotionMaestro, a unified motion generation framework that learns a shared representation for complete motions and heterogeneous partial observations through masked motion tokenization. MotionMaestro employs a three-stage training strategy that first learns a masked motion tokenizer, then refines its reconstruction ability on clean motions, and finally trains a conditional flow-matching generator in the learned latent space. Furthermore, we introduce an observation map and an observation loss to explicitly preserve provided motion conditions during generation. With this unified representation and conditioning mechanism, MotionMaestro supports text-guided and unconditional synthesis, pose conditioning and partial completion, temporal interpolation, trajectory control, and motion continuation. Experiments on the large-scale RoMo and MotionMillion datasets show state-of-the-art performance across diverse motion generation tasks.

</details>


### [166] [GeoSET: Generalist Foundation Model for SAR-to-EO Image Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37496)
*Jeonghyeok Do,Munchurl Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 GeoSET，首个面向 SAR-to-EO 图像翻译（SET）的通才模型，通过构建超 300 万高质量 SAR-EO 配对数据集并预训练条件生成器，实现对多种下游数据集的通用化适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 SAR-to-EO 图像翻译方法通常仅在单一、规模有限的数据集上训练，导致模型只能针对特定传感条件，缺乏跨传感器、跨分辨率和跨地域的泛化能力。

Method: 从超过 1000 万 SAR 观测中整理出 300 万余对高质量 SAR-EO 配对数据；设计斑噪鲁棒的 SAR 编码器以弥合 SAR 与预训练图像生成器之间的模态差异；在该异构数据上预训练条件生成器作为统一父模型，并采用 LoRA 仅更新 0.60% 的生成器参数进行高效下游适配。

Result: 在六个下游基准上，GeoSET 在全量微调或 LoRA 下均取得 FID 和 DISTS 的最优结果，每个数据集适配约需一小时。

Conclusion: GeoSET 验证了通用 SAR-to-EO 翻译的可行性，能够在异构 SAR-EO 域之间实现有效迁移，为跨传感条件的 SET 任务提供了统一范式。

Abstract: Paired synthetic aperture radar (SAR) and electro-optical (EO) imagery is increasingly available across sensors, resolutions, and geographic regions. Yet existing SAR-to-EO image translation (SET) methods are typically trained on a single, limited-scale dataset, producing models specialized to particular sensing conditions. We introduce GeoSET, the first generalist model for SET, built around a single pretrained parent that is adapted to downstream datasets under a common protocol. We curate over 3 million high-quality SAR--EO pairs from a collection of more than 10 million SAR observations, spanning diverse sensors, spatial resolutions, and ground sampling distances. To bridge the modality gap between SAR observations and a pretrained image generator, we develop a speckle-robust SAR encoder and pretrain the conditional generator on this heterogeneous corpus. The resulting parent supports efficient adaptation across downstream datasets through low-rank adaptation (LoRA), updating only 0.60% of the generator parameters and requiring approximately one hour per dataset. Across six downstream benchmarks, GeoSET achieves state-of-the-art results in FID and DISTS with full fine-tuning or LoRA, demonstrating effective transfer across heterogeneous SAR-EO domains.

</details>


### [167] [Principled MAP estimation for inverse problems: bridging the gap between convergence and performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37529)
*Alexandre Lagier,Valentine Tosel,Anne Gagneux,Mathurin Massias,Ségolène Martin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合递减噪声级去噪器与收敛性调度的算法，从贝叶斯角度证明其收敛到MAP估计，并在严重病态逆问题中兼具收敛性与高重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练去噪器可将图像先验融入恢复算法。Plug-and-Play与RED在有一阶优化收敛保证，但在严重病态逆问题上重建质量不足；基于流/扩散生成模型的去噪器沿递减噪声级评估，经验性能强却缺乏收敛理论。本文旨在弥合这一理论与实践间的差距。

Method: 专门设计一种算法，将递减噪声级下的去噪器与为收敛而设计的噪声调度相结合。

Result: 在合适假设下从贝叶斯角度证明该方法收敛到最大后验（MAP）估计；在多种病态逆问题上，性能超过有收敛保证的方法，并与当前最先进的经验方法相当。

Conclusion: 该工作为沿递减噪声级使用去噪器的算法提供了收敛理论支撑，同时保持了与最先进经验方法相当的重建性能。

Abstract: Pretrained denoisers provide a powerful way to incorporate image priors into restoration algorithms. Plug-and-Play and RED approaches exploit fixed-noise-level denoisers within first-order optimization schemes, with convergence guarantees, but often struggle to achieve high-quality reconstruction on severely ill-posed inverse problems. In contrast, recent state-of-the-art approaches leverage denoisers derived from flow- or diffusion-based generative models and evaluate them along a sequence of decreasing noise levels. While these methods achieve strong empirical performance, their convergence theory remains limited. In this paper, we bridge this gap by specifically designing an algorithm that combines denoisers at decreasing noise levels with a schedule tailored to ensure convergence. From a Bayesian perspective, we prove that our method converges to a $\textit{Maximum a Posteriori}$ (MAP) estimate, under suitable assumptions. Subsequently, we apply our method to various ill-posed inverse problems and show that it surpasses convergent methods while competing with state-of-the-art empirical ones.

</details>


### [168] [Weeding Out Bad Seeds: Initial-Noise-Robust Unlearning for Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37537)
*Arian Komaei Koma,Seyed Amir Kasaei,Aida Aryafar,Matin Ghiasi,Ali Aghayari,Amirhossein Souri,Mohammad Mosayyebi,AmirMahdi Sadeghzadeh,Mohammad Hossein Rohban*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示了T2I模型机器遗忘中的"概率性遗忘"问题，并提出自适应、概念条件化的噪声采样策略，显著提升遗忘鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前SOTA的机器遗忘方法对噪声初始化高度脆弱：被抑制的概念在特定随机初始噪声条件下会重新出现。作者将这一失败归因于标准高斯采样与遗忘目标之间的不匹配——目标概念仅出现在特定的初始噪声区域，而均匀随机采样产生稀疏、无信息的梯度更新，无法实现稳健的擦除。

Method: 提出一种自适应、概念条件化的采样策略，动态地将梯度更新集中到目标概念显现的区域，并降低无信息区域的权重。该框架与六种不同的SOTA遗忘方法集成，覆盖四种扩散骨干网络。

Result: 在安全性、物体和艺术风格遗忘任务中评估，并在黑盒与白盒对抗攻击下测试。相比四个基线，条件裸体内容在随机初始化下的重现率平均降低67.2%，同时降低了两种对抗评估下的攻击成功率。该方法在保持有竞争力的生成质量和目标擦除性能的同时，增强了对抗鲁棒性和非目标保留能力。

Conclusion: 自适应噪声采样有效解决了机器遗忘中的概率性遗忘问题，在多种方法、骨干和概念域上均提升了鲁棒性与擦除效果，为T2I模型的安全后处理提供了更可靠的方案。

Abstract: Machine unlearning has emerged as a critical post-hoc safety measure to erase sensitive concepts from Text-to-Image (T2I) models without prohibitive retraining. However, we reveal that current state-of-the-art (SOTA) approaches are brittle due to a severe lack of robustness to noise initialization. We call this phenomenon ``probabilistic forgetting'': suppressed concepts re-emerge under specific random initial noise conditions, despite appearing unlearned on other initializations. We trace this failure to the misalignment between standard Gaussian sampling during unlearning and the unlearning objective. Since the target concept manifests only in specific initial noise regions throughout the unlearning phase, uniform random sampling yields sparse, uninformative gradient updates that fail to drive robust erasure. To overcome this issue, we propose an adaptive, concept-conditioned sampling strategy that dynamically concentrates gradient updates on regions where the target concept manifests, down-weighting uninformative areas. We integrate our framework with six distinct SOTA unlearning methods across four diffusion backbones and evaluate it across safety, object, and artistic-style unlearning, as well as under black-box and white-box adversarial attacks. Our method reduces the conditional nudity re-emergence rate across random initializations by 67.2% on average over four baselines and lowers attack success rates across both adversarial evaluations. Across concept domains, Adaptive Noise Sampling strengthens adversarial robustness and non-target retention while preserving competitive generative quality and target-erasure performance.

</details>


### [169] [APM-Bench: Benchmarking Cross-session Persistent Memory for Egocentric Streaming Video Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37559)
*Jianguo Huang,Jinming Liu,Qiyao Wang,Liang Xu,Jianhang Li,Zhimian Wen,Mingda Li,Shule Lu,Zhicheng Wang,Yuhan Guo,Xin Jin,Wenjun Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出APM-Bench，将真实世界流式交互重构为多会话生活轨迹，用于评测持久记忆能力，揭示效用-延迟-存储的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流式视频基准和方法多聚焦单个连续视频或短视频片段，忽视了真实世界交互常是间歇性的、需要记忆跨中断持续存在的问题，因此缺乏针对持久记忆的评测平台。

Method: 提出APM-Bench，包含549个会话、104条轨迹和2719个候选样本，涵盖客观题与开放式问题；每个会话为带细粒度标注的视频，轨迹内会话围绕相关活动，模型需用持久记忆回答过去会话问题并主动响应。系统评测了不同记忆协议下的通用视频模型与多种专用流式记忆系统，并测试模型能否识别证据不足。

Result: 评测揭示出明显的效用-延迟-存储权衡：现有方法难以同时实现可靠的长期召回、低开销和有效的跨会话主动辅助。

Conclusion: APM-Bench为真实流式条件下开发与比较持久记忆系统提供了综合测试平台，希望推动未来同时考虑效用、延迟和存储的实用持久记忆研究。

Abstract: To serve as real-world personal assistants, streaming video models need persistent memory that retains past experiences for later use. Yet existing streaming benchmarks and methods often focus on individual continuous videos or short clips, overlooking that real-world interactions are often intermittent and require memory to persist across interruptions. To fill this gap, we introduce APM-Bench, which reformulates real-world streaming interaction as multi-session life trajectories. It contains 549 sessions, 104 trajectories, and 2,719 candidates, spanning both objective and open-ended questions. Each session is a video with fine-grained annotations, and sessions within a trajectory revolve around related activities. Models then use persistent memory to answer questions about past sessions and provide proactive responses while maintaining real-time interaction. This raises challenges: persistent memory must be storable, selectively retain information, be injected at the right time, and remain efficient. Moreover, finite storage may leave required evidence unavailable, so assistants should recognize missing evidence. Therefore, we systematically evaluate general video models under different memory protocols and diverse specialized streaming memory systems, and test whether models acknowledge insufficient evidence. Our evaluation reveals a clear utility--latency--storage trade-off: existing methods still struggle to simultaneously achieve reliable long-term recall, low overhead, and effective proactive assistance across sessions. APM-Bench provides a comprehensive testbed for developing and comparing persistent memory systems under realistic streaming conditions. We hope it encourages future work that jointly considers utility, latency, and storage toward more practical persistent memory for real-world streaming assistants.

</details>


### [170] [Decompose Radicals, Then Reward: Fine-Grained Inspection for Accurate Chinese Text Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37569)
*Yazhen Xie,Xingsong Ye,Zhineng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 IDSpect，用表意文字描述序列（IDS）替代 OCR 转录奖励，对中文渲染文本做部件级与空间关系级的细粒度奖励，提升文生图模型的中文字形结构质量与语义一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 OCR 的强化学习奖励将渲染出的中文文本解码后与目标字符串比对，把汉字当作原子字符处理，忽略了汉字由可复用部件按明确空间关系组合而成的构字本质。因此，视觉上不同的部首级错误可能得到同样粗糙的反馈，模型倾向于生成"形似"而非结构忠实的字形。

Method: 采用表意文字描述序列（IDS），由空间操作符与字符部件组成；训练一个专家级 IDS 识别器，将渲染出的中文文本转写为该表示。基于该识别器提出 IDSpect：将目标文本确定性地分解为 IDS token，并把裁剪级别的视觉 IDS 预测与目标序列对齐；利用全局唯一 token 信用，使比较对检测到的文本区域顺序具备鲁棒性。再与整体字符语义奖励结合，在不改动图像生成器、不增加推理成本的前提下提供部件与空间关系层面的细粒度信用。

Result: 在 Qwen-Image 上使用 GRPO 进行后训练的实验表明，IDSpect 在 LongText 与 GenTextEval 上取得了领先的结构质量与语义对齐表现。

Conclusion: 以 IDS 为基础的细粒度奖励能有效弥补 OCR 原子化评估的不足，在不增加推理开销的情况下显著改善中文文本渲染的结构保真度与语义一致性。

Abstract: Rendering accurate Chinese text remains challenging for text-to-image models. Existing OCR-based reinforcement-learning rewards compare decoded transcripts with target strings. Such rewards overlook the compositional nature of Chinese writing: an ideograph consists of reusable components arranged through explicit spatial relations, yet OCR evaluates it as an atomic character. Consequently, visually different radical-level errors may receive equally coarse feedback, encouraging glyphs that merely resemble the target instead of faithfully reproducing its internal structure. We employ Ideographic Description Sequences (IDS), which comprise spatial operators and character components, and train an expert IDS recognizer to transcribe rendered Chinese text into this representation. Building on this recognizer, we introduce IDSpect, which deterministically decomposes the target text into IDS tokens and aligns crop-level visual IDS predictions with the target sequence. Globally unique token credit makes this comparison robust to the order of detected text regions. Combined with a whole-character semantic reward, IDSpect supplies fine-grained credit with component and spatial-relation without changing the image generator or adding inference-time cost. Experiments with GRPO post-training of Qwen-Image demonstrate that IDSpect achieves leading structural quality and semantic alignment on LongText and GenTextEval.

</details>


### [171] [Evaluating the Evaluators: Diagnosing Large Multimodal Models for AI-Generated Image Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37576)
*Yu Zhao,Jiarui Wang,Huiyu Duan,Ye Zhao,Jutao Tang,Juntong Wang,Guangtao Zhai,Xiongkuo Min*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 SQUARE-Bench，一个从语义、质量、真实性、责任性四个维度系统评估大型多模态模型（LMM）作为 AI 生成图像评估器能力的综合基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
